- **strategy**: **Platform-native content &gt; cross-posted content** - Same content reformatted per platform outperforms identical cross-posts by 2-5x - LinkedIn: professional insight, first-person practitioner voice, long-form ok - Twitter/X: punchy, lowercase, hot takes, threads for depth - Newsletter: depth, exclusivity, personality, longer attention span - Each platform rewards different hooks, lengths, and for ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/channel-mix/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: Patterns from B2B outbound campaigns across SaaS, agencies, and consulting. Cold email/DM is a numbers game, but the right patterns compress the funnel. ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/cold-outbound/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: **Topical authority &gt; individual page optimization** - 20 interlinked articles on one topic outrank 20 scattered articles - Pillar page + cluster model: one hub (3000+ words) linked to 8-15 supporting pages - Internal linking between cluster pages signals depth to search engines ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/seo-engine/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: **Anchoring is the strongest lever** - Show the expensive option first.
